# Commands · S · part 11

1,372 entries, setup-project-adapters to ship. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **setup-project-adapters**: Re-sync tool adapters so every enabled CLI offers the same surface as Claude Code here. Trigger on 'add Cursor / Windsurf / Cline support to this repo', 're-sync the adapters', 'push my rule changes out to the other tools', 'make this repo work without .claude/ present'. Adapter files are derived projections — sinks, never sources. Do NOT trigger before .claude/ exists (/setup-project first), to change the SOURCE rules or commands (/setup-project --refine), or to promote adapter content back into .claude/, which is refused rather than merged. ([adnanmokhtar/refract](https://github.com/adnanmokhtar/refract/blob/HEAD/commands/setup-project-adapters.md) · ★2)

- **setup-project-facts**: Fill in .agents/project-facts.md, the one file the other agent commands read for anything about this project (where bugs are recorded, how to run the tests, what is a deliberate workaround). Reads the repo first and asks only what it cannot find. Run once after installing the commands, and again when a command stops because a bullet is unfilled. ([robertsolves/agent-commands](https://github.com/robertsolves/agent-commands/blob/HEAD/commands/setup-project-facts.md))
- **setup-project-health** ([adnanmokhtar/refract](https://github.com/adnanmokhtar/refract/blob/HEAD/tests/validators/lint-context-provenance.sh/bad/health-never-grades-it/commands/setup-project-health.md) · ★2)
- **setup-project-health**: Read-only report on whether this repo's /setup-project artifacts have drifted or gone stale. Dead files, budget breaches, missing ADRs, unconfirmed claims. Trigger on 'is my setup stale', 'check setup health', 'are the conventions still in sync with the code', 'why does /setup-project say there is no work to do'. Do NOT trigger on any ask to FIX drift (/setup-project --refresh) or when the caller expects a write of any kind. It grades setup artifacts, not product code — that is /audit. ([adnanmokhtar/refract](https://github.com/adnanmokhtar/refract/blob/HEAD/commands/setup-project-health.md) · ★2)
- **setup-project-health** ([adnanmokhtar/refract](https://github.com/adnanmokhtar/refract/blob/HEAD/tests/validators/lint-context-provenance.sh/bad/block-drops-unconfirmed/commands/setup-project-health.md) · ★2 · 4 copies)

- **setup-projects**: Configure the clients plugin for offerings, drive layout, companion-plugin integrations, and a derived SOW format template. Writes to `&lt;config-root&gt;/plugins/clients.user-context.md` and `&lt;config-root&gt;/plugins/clients.sow-template.md` using the shared vendor-neutral config-root resolver. Re-run anytime to add or update offerings; `--add-sample` merges a second SOW sample. ([BrightWayAI/clients](https://github.com/BrightWayAI/clients/blob/HEAD/commands/setup-projects.md))
- **setup-projeto**: Configura qualquer projeto (teu ou de cliente) com CLAUDE.md rico + reference/publico-alvo.md + reference/voz-&lt;handle&gt;.md (via /voz). DOIS MODOS + upgrade path — `fast` (8 perguntas, 5 min), `completo` (23 perguntas, 20 min), `completar` (detecta gaps e preenche). Default sem arg = pergunta qual modo. Use quando o usuário digitar "/setup-projeto", "configurar projeto", "novo projeto", "setup do projeto". ([ahoydig/dna-operacional](https://github.com/ahoydig/dna-operacional/blob/HEAD/plugins/dna-operacional/commands/setup-projeto.md))

- **shader**: A `&lt;shader&gt;` item is a GLSL ES 3.00 fragment shader. For its time range it reads the composite of every track beneath it and draws the frame that replaces it. Tracks above it draw over the result as usual, and several shaders stack in track order. Where nothing is beneath it, the composite is transparent black, so a shader can draw a whole frame on its own. ([MdSadiqMd/dotfiles](https://github.com/MdSadiqMd/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/agents/skills/cardboard/commands/shader.md))

- **shannon-gap**: Compute the link's **Strehl ratio** — its achieved spectral efficiency divided by the theoretical Shannon ceiling for the same SNR and bandwidth. In optics, Strehl is the achieved peak intensity over the diffraction-limited ideal; here it is achieved bits/Hz over the information-theoretic ideal. A perfect modem sits near 1.0; every dB you bleed to impairments drops it. ([DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab](https://github.com/DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab/blob/HEAD/satellite-communication-protocols/.claude/commands/shannon-gap.md) · ★1)

- **sheets-cell**: The detail view for **one cell**: what was entered, what it evaluates to, and — when it is a formula — the current values of every cell it references. `sheets read` is the list view; this is where an agent goes to find out *why* `D2` shows `#VALUE!`, or to confirm what a formula it wrote is actually computing from. One call, no scope beyond `spreadsheets`, read-only ([read-only-stays-read-only](.. ([JarvusInnovations/gws-axi](https://github.com/JarvusInnovations/gws-axi/blob/HEAD/specs/commands/sheets-cell.md) · ★10)
- **sheets-dimensions**: Structural edits that **shift data in place**: insert empty rows or columns at a position (everything below or to the right moves), or delete rows or columns (everything after them moves up or left). `sheets append` only ever adds rows at the end and `sheets clear` empties cells without moving anything; these four are the verbs for "make room in the middle" and "take this row out".
